Solved counts are out of the true and false cases, declined correctly out of the contingent ones (a contingent case is never solved: the right response there is no verdict); —: no such case.

Timing

Solved cases: 0.742 s in total, median 0.012 s per case, over 63 solved case(s) with a time. Times are wall-clock seconds per query as the runner measured them (each query in its own forked process; setup and warm-up not timed); totals and medians are over the solved cases only, so a timeout, error or no verdict never counts as a fast answer. They are shown next to the counts, never combined with them.

Measured on machine pi5 · indicative: it has some other load: times are a fair guide, large differences between its runs mean something, small ones may be noise. Raspberry Pi 5, 4 cores. Benchmark runs go one at a time, in a low-priority systemd unit (CPUWeight=20, Nice=10); since 2026-10-17, each eval is pinned to core 3 (earlier runs were not pinned). The Pi also serves websites, and other work may share core 3 unless it is reserved for the runs (pi5-deploy INSTALL.md, step 15). Clock frequency and temperature are not controlled.

Setup (run once per start, not counted in the per-query times)

measured setup time: 0.429 s

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x))
Residual = acosh(cosh(x)) - Abs(x)
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceacosh(cosh(x)) - Abs(x)→-Abs(x) + acosh(cosh(x))
askQ.zero(Residual)→None
answernull

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x))
Residual = Abs(x) / 6 - Abs(x / -6)
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceAbs(x) / 6 - Abs(x / -6)→0
askQ.zero(Residual)→True
answertrue

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(Abs(x)))
Residual = Abs(x) / x - (x / Abs(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceAbs(x) / x - (x / Abs(x))→-x/Abs(x) + Abs(x)/x
askQ.zero(Residual)→None
answernull

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Or(~Q.zero(x), re(pi) > 0))
Residual = Abs(Pow(x, pi)) - Pow(x, pi)
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

askQ.zero(Residual)→None

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), ~Q.zero(cosh(asinh(x))), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

askQ.zero(Residual)→None
answernull

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(x), Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False), Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False))
Residual = asin(cos(atan(x))) - Abs(atan(1 / x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

askQ.zero(Residual)→None

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), Ne(sinh(acosh(x)), I, evaluate=False), Ne(sinh(acosh(x)), -I, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cosh(acosh(x))))
Residual = atan(sinh(acosh(x))) - asin(tanh(acosh(x)))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True)
Assum = And(Q.complex(x), ~Q.zero(Abs(x)), ~Q.zero(x))
Residual = Pow(Abs(x), -2) - Pow(x, -2)
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)

# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
difference-Abs(atanh(x)) - atanh(-Abs(x))→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
askQ.zero(Residual)→None
answernull

Residual = 3 * log(x) - log(Pow(x, 3))

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:

from sympy import *
import sympy

# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')

# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

Assum = And(Q.real(x), ~Q.zero(x), ~Q.zero(Pow(x, 7)))
Residual = 7 * log(x) - log(Pow(x, 7))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
